<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rhapsodic</td><td>/ræpˈsɑːdɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂喜的，赞美不已的</td><td>rhapsodic praise<br>赞不绝口的夸奖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers were rhapsodic about the young cellist's tone.  评论者对这位年轻大提琴手的音色赞不绝口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crestfallen</td><td>/ˈkrestfɔːlən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂头丧气的，沮丧的</td><td>look utterly crestfallen<br>显得十分沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came home crestfallen after losing the final match by one point.  决赛以一分之差落败后，他垂头丧气地回了家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dejected</td><td>/dɪˈdʒektɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沮丧的，失望的</td><td>a dejected look<br>沮丧的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left the interview dejected and sure she had failed.  她面试后神情沮丧，确信自己失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depressed</td><td>/dɪˈprest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不开心的，情绪不高的，消沉的</td><td>feel deeply depressed<br>感到十分消沉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt depressed for weeks after the letter of rejection arrived.  收到拒信之后，她消沉了好几个星期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>rarefy</b></del></td><td>/ˈrerəfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使稀薄</td><td>rarefy the air<br>使空气稀薄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Great altitude rarefies the air and makes every step exhausting.  海拔高会使空气稀薄，让每一步都令人疲惫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attenuate</td><td>/əˈtenjueɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减弱，衰减；变细</td><td>attenuate the signal strength<br>减弱信号强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick concrete walls attenuate the radio signal inside the building.  厚厚的混凝土墙削弱了建筑内部的无线电信号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dilute</td><td>/daɪˈluːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>稀释，冲淡；经稀释的</td><td>dilute the acid<br>稀释这种酸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please dilute the concentrated juice with three parts water before serving it.  上桌前请用三倍的水稀释这种浓缩果汁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thin</td><td>/ˈθɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使变稀；薄的，瘦的</td><td>thin the paint slightly<br>把颜料稍微稀释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin the sauce with a little warm water before serving.  上菜前用少量温水把酱汁调稀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concentrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condense</td><td>/kənˈdens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，缩短；使凝结</td><td>condense the article<br>压缩这篇文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to condense three chapters into a single clear page.  她把三章内容压缩成清晰的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rash</b></td><td>/ræʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>草率的，仓促的</td><td>a rash decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He regretted his rash decision to sell the house so quickly.  他后悔自己如此草率地决定迅速卖掉房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gadarene</del></td><td>/ˈɡædəˌrin/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>鲁莽疾冲的，一哄而上的</td><td>a gadarene rush<br>一哄而上的疾冲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a gadarene rush for the exits when the lights failed.  灯一灭，人们一哄而上冲向出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headlong</td><td>/ˈhedlɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>鲁莽的，草率的</td><td>rush headlong into<br>贸然冲进…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their headlong rush to publish left several errors in the final report.  他们急于发表的草率做法使最终报告留下了几处错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurried</td><td>/ˈhɜːrid/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，仓促的</td><td>a hurried decision<br>仓促做出的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hurried decision to sell cost them a great deal.  他们仓促决定出售，代价惨重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overhasty</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈheɪsti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于匆忙的，草率的</td><td>an overhasty judgment<br>过于草率的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their overhasty decision to expand nearly ruined the young company.  他们草率的扩张决定几乎毁了这家年轻公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitous</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常陡峭的；匆忙的</td><td>a precipitous decline<br>急剧的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path became precipitous near the summit, forcing us to use ropes.  靠近峰顶时山路变得非常陡峭，迫使我们用上绳索。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rushed</td><td>/ˈrʌʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，草率的</td><td>a rushed decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report felt rushed and full of careless mistakes.  这份报告显得草率，满是粗心的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>raspy</b></td><td>/ˈræspi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音刺耳的</td><td>a raspy voice<br>沙哑刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of smoking had left him with a low, raspy voice.  多年吸烟使他的嗓音低沉而刺耳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cacophonous</td><td>/kəˈkɑfənəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，不和谐的</td><td>a cacophonous racket<br>刺耳的喧闹声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra produced a cacophonous din while tuning up.  乐队调音时发出刺耳的嘈杂声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coarse</td><td>/kɔːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，表面不平整的；粗俗的</td><td>coarse language<br>粗俗的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers used coarse language that shocked the visiting students.  工人们说的粗话让来访的学生们大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 croaky</td><td>/ˈkroʊki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音低哑的</td><td>a croaky voice<br>低哑的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered in a croaky voice after two nights of coughing.  咳了两晚后她用低哑的嗓音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harsh</td><td>/hɑːrʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>harsh criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's harsh sentence surprised even the prosecutors in the courtroom.  法官严厉的判决连法庭上的检察官都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grating</td><td>/ˈɡreɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，令人不适的</td><td>a grating voice<br>刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The grating noise of the saw filled the workshop.  锯子刺耳的声音充满了作坊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gravelly</td><td>/ˈɡrævəli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音低沉粗糙的</td><td>a gravelly voice<br>粗哑的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old singer's gravelly voice suited the sad song perfectly.  老歌手粗哑的嗓音与这首悲伤的歌极为契合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gruff</td><td>/ɡrʌf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>生硬粗暴的；声音低沉的</td><td>a gruff voice<br>低沉粗哑的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gruff old sailor pointed silently toward the harbor entrance.  一位声音低沉的老水手默默指向港口入口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 husky</td><td>/ˈhʌski/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（尤指声音）沙哑的，粗糙的</td><td>a husky voice<br>沙哑的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long concert his voice was husky and barely audible.  漫长的演唱会后，他的声音沙哑，几乎听不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rasping</td><td>/ˈræspɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刺耳的，沙哑的</td><td>a rasping cough<br>沙哑的咳嗽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rasping whisper came from behind the heavy curtain.  厚窗帘后传来一声沙哑的低语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rusty</td><td>/ˈrʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音生涩沙哑的</td><td>a rusty voice<br>生涩的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of silence his voice sounded rusty and thin.  多年不语后，他的声音听起来生涩而单薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scratchy</td><td>/ˈskrætʃi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沙沙作响的，刺耳的</td><td>a scratchy recording<br>沙沙响的录音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scratchy old record still filled the room with warmth.  那张沙沙作响的老唱片仍让房间充满温暖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 throaty</td><td>/ˈθroʊti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喉音重的，低哑的</td><td>a throaty laugh<br>低哑的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered with a throaty laugh that filled the hallway.  她以一阵低哑的笑声作答，笑声充满走廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mellifluous</td><td>/meˈlɪfluəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（声音）甜美流畅的</td><td>a mellifluous voice<br>甜美悦耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The narrator's mellifluous voice made even the dullest chapters pleasant to listen to.  叙述者优美悦耳的嗓音让最枯燥的章节也变得动听。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sweet</td><td>/ˈswiːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>甜的；温和的，亲切的</td><td>in a sweet tone<br>以温和的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a sweet voice that calmed the frightened child.  她用温柔的声音说话，安抚了害怕的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ratify</b></td><td>/ˈrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（官方地）认可，批准</td><td>ratify the peace treaty<br>批准和平条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate voted to ratify the peace treaty after weeks of debate.  经过数周辩论，参议院投票批准了和平条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accredit</td><td>/əˈkredɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认可，授权；归功于</td><td>accredit the university<br>认可该大学资质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally accredited the new nursing program.  委员会终于认可了这个新的护理专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 approbate</del></td><td>/ˈæprəbeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞许，认可</td><td>approbate the decision<br>认可这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board approbated the merger after a long private debate.  经过长时间闭门辩论，董事会认可了这次合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorize</td><td>/ˈɔːθəˌraɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>授权，批准，委托</td><td>authorize the payment<br>批准这笔付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the director can authorize spending above that limit.  只有主管能批准超出该限额的开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certify</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明，认证</td><td>certify the results as valid<br>证明结果有效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An independent lab must certify that the water is safe.  必须由独立实验室证明水质安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finalize</td><td>/ˈfaɪnəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>最终确定，敲定</td><td>finalize the agreement<br>敲定协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides hope to finalize the agreement before December.  双方希望在十二月前敲定协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 formalize</td><td>/ˈfɔːrməˌlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使正式化，使规范化</td><td>formalize the agreement<br>使协议正式化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms decided to formalize their loose partnership.  两家公司决定把松散的合作正式化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass</td><td>/ˈpæs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>通过（法案）；经过</td><td>pass a controversial bill<br>通过一项有争议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate passed the budget after two nights of debate.  经过两晚辩论，参议院通过了预算案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disallow</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈlaʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不许，驳回</td><td>disallow the goal<br>判进球无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee disallowed the goal for an obvious offside.  裁判因明显越位判该进球无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disapprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈpruːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不赞成，反对</td><td>disapprove of the plan<br>不赞成该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents strongly disapprove of the plan to drop out.  她父母强烈不赞成退学的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negative</td><td>/ˈneɡətɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>否决，反驳；负面的</td><td>negative the proposal<br>否决这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee negatived the motion after a short debate.  委员会经短暂辩论后否决了该动议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veto</td><td>/ˈviːtoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>否决，禁止</td><td>veto the proposed bill<br>否决拟议的法案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The governor promised to veto the bill if it reached his desk.  州长承诺，若这项法案送到他手上就予以否决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>ratiocination</b></del></td><td>/ˌræʃioʊsəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>推理</td><td>careful ratiocination<br>缜密的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The detective solved the case through careful ratiocination rather than luck.  这位侦探靠缜密的推理而非运气破了案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deduction</td><td>/dɪˈdʌkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>推论，演绎</td><td>logical deduction<br>逻辑推演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By simple deduction she knew the letter had been opened.  通过简单推论她就知道信被打开过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 intellection</del></td><td>/ˌɪntəˈlɛkʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>思考，理性思维</td><td>pure intellection<br>纯粹的思辨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He treats mathematics as pure intellection, untouched by the world.  他把数学看作不涉世事的纯粹思辨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasoning</td><td>/ˈriːzənɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>推理，论证</td><td>sound reasoning<br>严密的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her reasoning was clear enough for the jury to follow.  她的推理清楚得陪审团都能跟上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sense</td><td>/ˈsens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>意思，含义；感觉</td><td>in a broad sense<br>广义上讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In a broad sense, every artist borrows from earlier traditions.  广义上讲，每位艺术家都借鉴前人的传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ration</b></td><td>/ˈræʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>定量分配，配给</td><td>ration the water supply<br>定量分配用水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city had to ration the water supply during the long drought.  长期干旱期间，城市不得不定量分配供水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allocate</td><td>/ˈæləˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分配，划拨</td><td>allocate funds fairly<br>公平划拨资金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council allocated most of the grant to school repairs.  议会把大部分拨款划给了校舍维修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allot</td><td>/əˈlɑːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分配，配给</td><td>allot ten minutes each<br>每人分配十分钟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each speaker was allotted exactly seven minutes.  每位发言者被分配到整整七分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allow</td><td>/əˈlaʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>允许，准许</td><td>allow visitors inside<br>允许访客进入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library allows visitors to photograph the manuscripts without flash.  图书馆允许访客不开闪光灯拍摄手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apportion</td><td>/əˈpɔːrʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>按比例分配，分摊</td><td>apportion the costs<br>按比例分摊费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The repair costs were apportioned among all six tenants.  维修费用在六位租户之间按比例分摊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assign</td><td>/əˈsaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，分配；指定</td><td>assign a difficult task<br>指派一项难任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor assigned the story to her most careful reporter.  编辑把这篇报道指派给最细心的记者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distribute</td><td>/dɪsˈtrɪbjut/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分发，分配</td><td>distribute the supplies<br>分发物资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers distributed blankets to every family by nightfall.  志愿者在天黑前给每家分发了毛毯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lot</td><td>/ˈlɑːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一批，一群，一堆</td><td>a bad lot<br>一帮坏人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The auction sold the whole lot for very little.  拍卖会把这一整批以极低价卖掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retain</td><td>/rɪˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保留，留住</td><td>retain the original title<br>保留原名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum retained the building's original facade during the renovation.  翻修时博物馆保留了建筑的原有正面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 withhold</td><td>/wɪðˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制；扣压，不给予</td><td>withhold information<br>隐瞒信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of withholding key information.  他被指控隐瞒关键信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rational</b></td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 commonsensical</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈsensɪkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎常理的，明智的</td><td>a commonsensical approach<br>合乎常理的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His advice was commonsensical: sleep more and worry less.  他的建议合乎常理：多睡觉，少操心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 just</td><td>/ˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>公正的，公平的；仅仅</td><td>a just decision<br>公正的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone agreed the settlement was a just outcome.  所有人都认为这项和解是公正的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Justified</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有正当理由的，合理的</td><td>fully justified anger<br>完全合理的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her complaint was justified, and the store refunded the money.  她的投诉有理，商店退了钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 levelheaded</td><td>/ˌlevlˈhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>头脑冷静的，明智的</td><td>a levelheaded response<br>明智冷静的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her levelheaded response to the emergency probably saved several lives that night.  她对紧急情况明智冷静的回应那晚可能救了好几条命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solid</td><td>/ˈsɑːlɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗壮结实的</td><td>a solid, heavy build<br>粗壮结实的体格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wrestler had a solid, heavy build unlike his slender opponent.  这名摔跤手体格粗壮结实，与瘦削的对手截然不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 groundless</td><td>/ˈɡraʊndləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有依据的</td><td>groundless accusations<br>毫无根据的指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court found the accusations entirely groundless and dismissed them.  法庭认定这些指控毫无根据，予以驳回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfounded</td><td>/ənˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无根据的</td><td>unfounded rumors<br>毫无根据的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company called the reports of bankruptcy completely unfounded.  公司称破产的报道完全没有根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unjustified</td><td>/ʌnˈdʒʌstəfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无正当理由的</td><td>an unjustified attack<br>无理的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harsh criticism of her work was completely unjustified.  对她工作的严厉批评完全没有道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rave</b></td><td>/reɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>狂热赞扬；（发疯般地）怒吼</td><td>rave about the film<br>对影片赞不绝口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics raved about the film for weeks after its quiet premiere.  影片低调首映后，评论家连续数周对它赞不绝口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drool</td><td>/ˈdruːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>垂涎；过分夸赞</td><td>drool over the new car<br>对新车垂涎不已</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans drooled over every photo of the new sports car.  粉丝们对新跑车的每张照片都垂涎不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 effuse</del></td><td>/ɪˈfjuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>倾泻，热情倾吐</td><td>effuse warmth and praise<br>倾泻般地热情赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She effused praise for every painting in the small gallery.  她对小画廊里的每幅画都赞不绝口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthuse</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>热情地说；使热情</td><td>enthuse about the trip<br>热情谈论这次旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enthused about the trip for the entire evening.  他整晚都在热情地谈这次旅行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 rhapsodize</del></td><td>/ˈræpsəˌdaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>热情地谈论，赞不绝口</td><td>rhapsodize about the food<br>对食物赞不绝口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rhapsodized about the little bakery for a full hour.  他对那家小面包房赞不绝口地讲了整整一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slobber</td><td>/ˈslɑːbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>流口水；过分表露感情</td><td>slobber over the baby<br>对婴儿过分亲热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The big dog slobbered all over my clean white shirt.  那只大狗把口水流满了我干净的白衬衫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bluster</td><td>/ˈblʌstər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>狂妄自大地大声说；喧闹的状态</td><td>bluster and threaten<br>大声吓唬威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager began to bluster and threaten when questioned closely.  被仔细追问时，经理开始狂妄自大地大声吓唬威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fulminate</td><td>/ˈfʌlmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>大声斥责</td><td>fulminate against corruption<br>大声斥责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor fulminated against corruption in every weekly column.  那位老编辑在每周的专栏里大声斥责腐败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 huff</td><td>/ˈhʌf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>气恼，发怒</td><td>leave in a huff<br>气冲冲地离开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left in a huff when nobody supported her proposal.  没人支持她的提议，她气冲冲地走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rant</td><td>/rænt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>（尤指长时间的）训斥，责骂</td><td>go on a rant<br>大发一通牢骚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach went on a long rant about the team's careless defending.  教练就球队漫不经心的防守训斥了很久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roar</td><td>/ˈrɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>咆哮，怒吼；轰鸣</td><td>roar with anger<br>愤怒地咆哮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach roared at the players from the sideline.  教练在场边对球员们大声咆哮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spout</td><td>/ˈspaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喷出，涌出；喷口</td><td>spout hot water<br>喷出热水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whale surfaced and spouted a tall column of mist.  鲸鱼浮出水面，喷出一道高高的水雾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blame</td><td>/ˈbleɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>责备，归咎于</td><td>blame someone for something<br>因某事责怪某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't blame me for the broken window.  别把窗户破了怪在我头上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condemn</td><td>/kənˈdem/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责（…为不道德的、邪恶的）</td><td>condemn the attack<br>谴责这次袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 World leaders quickly condemned the attack on the hospital.  各国领导人迅速谴责了对医院的袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 criticize</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪˌsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>批评，指责；评论</td><td>criticize the decision<br>批评这个决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several members criticized the plan for ignoring small farmers.  几名成员批评该计划忽视了小农。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pan</td><td>/pæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>严厉批评</td><td>pan a new film<br>严厉批评一部新片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics panned the film for its clumsy dialogue and predictable ending.  影评人严厉批评这部电影对话笨拙、结局可预料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 reprehend</del></td><td>/reprɪˈhend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，指责</td><td>reprehend such conduct<br>谴责这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee did not hesitate to reprehend the officer for his careless report.  委员会毫不犹豫地谴责了那名官员草率的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grumble</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指低声地）抱怨，埋怨</td><td>grumble under one's breath<br>低声抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers grumbled about the new rules but followed them anyway.  工人们低声抱怨新规定，但还是照办了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mutter</td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ravel</b></td><td>/ˈrævl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，松开；阐明</td><td>ravel out the thread<br>把线头解开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She patiently began to ravel out the tangled thread with a needle.  她耐心地用针把缠住的线头解开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disentangle</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtæŋɡəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，理清，使脱离</td><td>disentangle the wires<br>把电线理清</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took hours to disentangle fact from rumor in the report.  在这份报告中把事实与谣言分开花了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extricate</td><td>/ˈekstrɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使解脱，救出</td><td>extricate himself from debt<br>使自己脱离债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him years to extricate himself from serious debt.  他花了好几年才使自己脱离沉重债务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unbraid</del></td><td>/ʌnˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开（辫子），松开</td><td>unbraid her long hair<br>解开她的长辫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She unbraided her hair slowly in front of the small mirror.  她在小镜子前慢慢解开辫子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unsnarl</del></td><td>/ʌnˈsnɑrl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开缠结，理清</td><td>unsnarl the tangled wires<br>理清缠住的电线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took an hour to unsnarl the fishing line.  理清那根钓鱼线花了一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 untwine</del></td><td>/ʌnˈtwaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开缠绕，松开</td><td>untwine the two ropes<br>把两根绳解开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He patiently untwined the vine from the iron gate.  他耐心地把藤蔓从铁门上解下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unweave</del></td><td>/ʌnˈwiv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拆解（织物），解开</td><td>unweave the old basket<br>拆开旧篮子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Restorers must carefully unweave the damaged border before repairing the tapestry.  修复者必须小心拆开破损的边缘，然后再修补这幅壁毯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elucidate</td><td>/iˈluːsɪdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>elucidate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The footnotes elucidate the problem far better than the chapter itself does.  脚注比正文本身更好地阐明了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untangle</td><td>/ʌnˈtæŋɡəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开纠结，理清</td><td>untangle the knot<br>解开这个结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The auditors are still trying to untangle the company's finances.  审计人员仍在努力理清这家公司的财务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unravel</td><td>/ənˈrævəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解开，拆散；弄清，解决</td><td>unravel the mystery<br>解开谜团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Detectives worked for months to unravel the tangled web of lies.  侦探们花了几个月才解开这张谎言之网。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complex</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpleks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>复杂的；综合体</td><td>a complex argument<br>复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper presents a complex argument about ancient trade routes.  这篇论文提出了关于古代商路的复杂论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entangle</td><td>/ɪnˈtæŋɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使缠住，使卷入；使复杂</td><td>entangle the negotiations<br>使谈判变复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New objections entangled the negotiations and delayed the deal for weeks.  新的反对意见使谈判陷入纠缠，把交易推迟了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perplex</td><td>/pərˈpleks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使费解</td><td>perplex the researchers<br>使研究者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange results perplexed the researchers for several long months.  奇怪的结果让研究者困惑了好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticate</td><td>/səˈfɪstəˌkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使复杂化；老于世故者</td><td>sophisticate the design further<br>把设计进一步复杂化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers sophisticated the model until nobody could explain it.  工程师把模型改得越来越复杂，最后没人能解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snarl</td><td>/snɑːrl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>纠缠，纠结</td><td>snarled traffic<br>堵成一团的车流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow snarled traffic across the city for most of the morning.  大雪使全城交通整个上午陷入混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangle</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>纠缠，使…纠结</td><td>tangle the fishing line<br>把鱼线弄缠住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wind tangled the fishing line around the branches overhead.  风把鱼线缠在头顶的树枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 braid</td><td>/ˈbreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>编成辫子；辫子，饰带</td><td>braid her hair<br>把她的头发编成辫子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother would braid her long hair every morning before school.  她奶奶每天早上上学前都会给她编辫子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 knit</td><td>/nɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>连接，联系</td><td>knit two communities together<br>把两个社区联结起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new festival helped knit two divided communities closely together again.  这个新节庆帮助把两个分裂的社区重新紧密联结起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plait</td><td>/ˈpleɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>编成辫；发辫，褶</td><td>plait the straw<br>把稻草编在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weaver taught us how to plait dried reeds into sturdy baskets.  那位编织匠教我们把干芦苇编成结实的篮子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weave</td><td>/ˈwiːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>编织，纺织；穿行</td><td>weave a basket<br>编一个篮子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers still weave cloth on wooden looms handed down for generations.  村民们仍用世代相传的木织机织布。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ravish</b></td><td>/ˈrævɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使陶醉，使沉迷</td><td>ravish the eye with color<br>用色彩令人陶醉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painter's bold colors ravish the eye and hold it captive.  这位画家大胆的色彩令人赏心悦目、目不转睛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 captivate</td><td>/ˈkæptɪveɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷，深深吸引</td><td>captivate the audience<br>深深吸引观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel captivated readers around the world.  她的第一部小说令世界各地的读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enchant</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使入迷，使陶醉</td><td>be enchanted by the scenery<br>被风景迷住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old fishing village enchanted every visitor who stayed there.  那个古老渔村令每位住过的游客陶醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enrapture</del></td><td>/ɪnˈræptʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使狂喜，使着迷</td><td>enrapture the whole audience<br>使全场观众入迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist enraptured everyone in the small concert hall.  这位年轻钢琴家让小音乐厅里的每个人都入了迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthrall</td><td>/ɪnˈθrɔːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（像用魔咒般）吸引</td><td>enthrall the entire audience<br>吸引住全场观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magician enthralled the entire audience with a single deck of cards.  魔术师仅用一副牌就吸引住了全场观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapture</td><td>/ˈræptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>极度欢喜，狂喜</td><td>listen in rapture<br>如痴如醉地聆听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience listened in rapture as the old singer began her final song.  老歌手唱起最后一曲时，观众听得如痴如醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disenchant</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使清醒</td><td>disenchant the young voters<br>使年轻选民清醒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years in politics disenchanted her with grand promises.  多年从政使她对宏大承诺不再抱幻想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>raze</b></td><td>/reɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底摧毁，拆为平地</td><td>raze a village<br>将村庄彻底摧毁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invading army razed the village and burned every field around it.  入侵军队将村庄彻底摧毁，并烧掉了周围所有田地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annihilate</td><td>/əˈnaɪəˌleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底摧毁，歼灭</td><td>annihilate the enemy<br>歼灭敌人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single flood could annihilate the entire harvest of the valley.  一场洪水就能彻底毁掉整个山谷的收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decimate</td><td>/ˈdesəˌmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>大量毁灭，大批杀死</td><td>decimate the population<br>使人口大量减少</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The epidemic decimated herds of cattle across three provinces.  这场疫病使三个省的牛群大量死亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demolish</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拆除，摧毁；驳倒</td><td>demolish an old building<br>拆除旧楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city decided to demolish the unsafe old building last spring.  去年春天市政府决定拆除这栋危旧建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devastate</td><td>/ˈdevəˌsteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>毁灭，破坏；使震惊</td><td>devastate the coastal town<br>摧毁那座海滨小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The earthquake devastated neighborhoods that had stood for a century.  地震摧毁了矗立了一个世纪的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 level</td><td>/ˈlevəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>水平的，平的</td><td>on level ground<br>在平地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pitch the tent on level ground away from the stream.  把帐篷搭在远离溪水的平地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nuke</td><td>/ˈnuːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>用核武器攻击；彻底摧毁</td><td>nuke a city<br>对一座城市发动核打击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Generals debated whether to nuke the island rather than invade it.  将军们争论是否用核武器打击该岛而非登陆进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pulverize</td><td>/ˈpʌlvəraɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>将…研磨成粉；将…彻底摧毁</td><td>pulverize the rock<br>把岩石磨成粉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers used heavy hammers to pulverize the rock into fine powder.  工人用重锤把岩石研磨成细粉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruin</td><td>/ˈruːɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>毁坏，毁灭；废墟</td><td>ruin the harvest<br>毁掉收成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of drought ruined the harvest and emptied the village granary.  数周干旱毁掉收成，村里粮仓也空了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smash</td><td>/ˈsmæʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>轰动的成功；打碎</td><td>a box-office smash<br>票房大热之作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The low-budget comedy became a box-office smash overnight.  这部低成本喜剧一夜之间成为票房大热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vaporize</td><td>/ˈveɪpəraɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底消灭</td><td>vaporize the target<br>把目标彻底毁灭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single blast vaporized the abandoned tower on the distant hill.  一次爆炸就把远处山上那座废弃塔楼彻底摧毁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wreck</td><td>/ˈrek/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>残骸；失事；破坏</td><td>the wreck of a ship<br>一艘船的残骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers photographed the wreck lying deep on the sandy bottom.  潜水员拍摄了深卧沙底的残骸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tear down</td><td>/tɛr ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拆毁，推倒；诋毁</td><td>tear down the old factory<br>拆掉那座旧工厂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to tear down the crumbling stadium next spring.  市议会投票决定明年春天拆除这座破败的体育场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 build</td><td>/ˈbɪld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>建造，建立；体格</td><td>build a bridge<br>建造一座桥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They plan to build a new library on the empty lot downtown.  他们计划在市中心的空地上建一座新图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 construct</td><td>/kənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>建造，建立；创造，想出</td><td>construct a theory<br>构建一套理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer constructed a careful argument from a handful of ordinary documents.  律师用少量普通文件构建出一套严密的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 erect</td><td>/ɪˈrekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>直立的，垂直的</td><td>stand erect<br>笔直地站着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old soldier still stood perfectly erect.  这位老兵仍站得笔直。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rear</td><td>/ˈrɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>养育，培养；竖立，后部</td><td>rear a large family<br>养育一大家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had to rear four children alone after her husband died.  丈夫去世后她只能独自养育四个孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 set up</td><td>/sɛt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>建立，创办；安装，搭建</td><td>set up a company<br>创办一家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up a small company in the garage two years ago.  两年前他们在车库里创办了一家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>row</b></td><td>/raʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>争吵，争执</td><td>have a row with him<br>和他吵一架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had a furious row with her landlord about the unpaid repairs.  她和房东就未付款的维修大吵了一架。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 altercation</td><td>/ˌɑːltərˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>激烈争吵，口角</td><td>a heated altercation<br>一场激烈的争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief altercation over parking escalated into a lawsuit.  一场关于停车的短暂争吵最后升级成了诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 controversy</td><td>/ˈkɑːntrəˌvɜːrsi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>争论，争议</td><td>spark a controversy<br>引发一场争议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting sparked a controversy that lasted for decades.  这幅画引发了持续数十年的争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagreement</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不一致，争执</td><td>a sharp disagreement<br>激烈的分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long disagreement over funding stalled the whole project.  关于资金的长期分歧使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispute</td><td>/dɪˈspjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>争论，争端；对…提出质疑</td><td>settle a dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighbors are still in dispute over the fence.  邻居们仍在为那道围栏争执。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissension</td><td>/dɪˈsenʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意见分歧，不和</td><td>internal dissension<br>内部分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internal dissension over funding split the small research group into two camps.  关于经费的内部分歧把这个小研究组分成了两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fight</td><td>/ˈfaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>对抗，斗争；打斗</td><td>fight the proposal<br>反对这项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The residents will fight the proposal to widen the old highway.  居民们将反对拓宽这条旧公路的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imbroglio</td><td>/ɪmˈbroʊlioʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，复杂的局面</td><td>a political imbroglio<br>政治上的乱局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister resigned in the middle of a diplomatic imbroglio.  部长在一场外交乱局之中辞职了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quarrel</td><td>/ˈkwɔːrəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>争吵，口角</td><td>pick a quarrel<br>找人吵架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers had a bitter quarrel about their father's will.  兄弟俩为父亲的遗嘱激烈争吵了一场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squabble</td><td>/ˈskwɑːbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>为琐事争吵；口角</td><td>squabble over money<br>为钱争吵不休</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children squabble endlessly about who sits by the window.  孩子们为谁坐窗边没完没了地吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrangle</td><td>/ˈræŋɡl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>纷争，争端</td><td>a legal wrangle<br>一场法律纷争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long legal wrangle over the land delayed construction for years.  一场关于这块土地的漫长法律纷争使工程推迟数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accord</td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consensus</td><td>/kənˈsensəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致同意</td><td>reach a consensus<br>达成一致意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally reached a consensus after four hours of debate.  董事会经过四小时辩论终于达成了一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unanimity</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈnɪməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全体一致，一致同意</td><td>rare unanimity<br>难得的一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was surprising unanimity among the usually divided members.  在平时意见分歧的成员中出现了惊人的一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unison</td><td>/ˈjuːnəsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致；齐声，同时</td><td>in unison<br>齐声地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children answered in unison when the teacher asked.  老师提问时孩子们齐声回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reactionary</b></td><td>/riˈækʃəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反对变革的，极保守的</td><td>reactionary policies<br>反对变革的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the new rules reactionary and openly unfair.  批评者称新规定保守倒退且明显不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 brassbound</del></td><td>/ˈbræsˌbaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固守旧的</td><td>a brassbound tradition<br>僵化的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brassbound committee refused to consider any new idea.  这个顽固守旧的委员会拒绝考虑任何新想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 die-hard</td><td>/ˈdaɪ hɑrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>顽固不化的；死硬派</td><td>a die-hard supporter<br>一个死忠支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a few die-hard fans stayed until the final whistle.  只有几个死忠球迷留到终场哨响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hidebound</td><td>/ˈhaɪdbaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>死板的，极度保守的</td><td>hidebound traditions<br>僵化的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hidebound innkeeper flatly refused to install internet for guests.  这位死板的旅店老板断然拒绝为客人装网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 traditionalistic</del></td><td>/trəˌdɪʃənəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，因循传统的</td><td>a traditionalistic society<br>一个守旧的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His traditionalistic views made him distrust every new teaching method.  他守旧的观念使他不信任任何新教法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ultraconservative</td><td>/ˌʌltrəkənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极端保守的；极端保守者</td><td>an ultraconservative faction<br>极端保守的派别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party's ultraconservative wing blocked every proposed reform.  该党极端保守的一翼阻挠了每项改革提议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ream</b></td><td>/riːm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>怒斥，训斥</td><td>ream out a recruit<br>痛骂一名新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sergeant reamed out the recruit for arriving late a third time.  中士痛骂那名第三次迟到的新兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baste</td><td>/ˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>痛打；严厉批评</td><td>baste him with criticism<br>用批评痛击他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist basted the mayor for wasting so much public money.  专栏作家痛批市长浪费了大量公款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 berate</td><td>/bɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉指责，斥责</td><td>berate him publicly<br>公开斥责他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach began to berate his players in front of reporters.  教练开始在记者面前严厉斥责他的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chastise</td><td>/tʃæˈstaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评，责骂</td><td>chastise for laziness<br>因懒惰而受责骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach chastised the players for their careless passing.  教练严厉批评球员们传球草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hammer</td><td>/ˈhæmər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屡次重申</td><td>hammer home the point<br>反复强调这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach hammered home the importance of defense at every single practice.  教练在每一次训练中都反复强调防守的重要性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rail</td><td>/reɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>痛斥，抱怨</td><td>rail against injustice<br>猛烈抨击不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students railed against the unfair new grading policy all week.  学生们整周都在痛斥不公平的新评分政策。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebuke</td><td>/rɪˈbjuːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>斥责，责难</td><td>a sharp rebuke<br>严厉的斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge rebuked the lawyer for interrupting the witness.  法官斥责律师打断证人发言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 upbraid</del></td><td>/ʌpˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（严厉地）谴责，责骂</td><td>upbraid him for lying<br>因撒谎而责骂他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager upbraided him for missing three deadlines in one month.  经理因他一个月内三次误期而严厉责骂他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accredit</td><td>/əˈkredɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认可，授权；归功于</td><td>accredit the university<br>认可该大学资质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally accredited the new nursing program.  委员会终于认可了这个新的护理专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eulogize</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>称赞；颂扬</td><td>eulogize the late leader<br>颂扬已故领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colleagues gathered to eulogize the scientist who had trained them all.  同事们聚在一起颂扬这位培养了他们所有人的科学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ramification</b></td><td>/ˌræmɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>影响，结果</td><td>far-reaching ramifications<br>影响深远的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few politicians considered the far-reaching ramifications of the sudden tax cut.  很少有政客考虑到突然减税影响深远的后果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequence</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结果；重要性，价值</td><td>of little consequence<br>无关紧要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full consequences of the new policy will not be clear for years.  这项新政策的全部后果多年内都不会明朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outgrowth</td><td>/ˈaʊtɡroʊθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结果，后果</td><td>a natural outgrowth of<br>…的自然结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new festival was a natural outgrowth of the summer concerts.  这个新节庆是夏季音乐会的自然产物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effect</td><td>/ɪˈfekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>影响，效果；实现</td><td>have a lasting effect<br>产生持久影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule had little effect on daily traffic.  新规定对日常交通几乎没有影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 result</td><td>/rɪˈzʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>结果；导致，产生</td><td>as a result of<br>由于…的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay resulted from a simple error in the schedule.  这次延误源于日程上的一个小错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reassure</b></td><td>/ˌriːəˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使安心，打消疑虑</td><td>reassure worried parents<br>安抚焦虑的父母</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal tried to reassure worried parents after the accident.  事故之后，校长努力安抚焦虑的父母。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assure</td><td>/əˈʃʊr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使确信，向…保证</td><td>assure him of support<br>向他保证支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager assured us that the missing files were safe.  经理向我们保证，丢失的文件很安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 console</td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solace</td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trouble</td><td>/ˈtrʌbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>麻烦，打扰；使烦恼</td><td>trouble you for a moment<br>打扰你一会儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 May I trouble you for directions to the train station?  能麻烦你告诉我去火车站的路吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rebuff</b></td><td>/rɪˈbʌf/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严词拒绝</td><td>rebuff the offer<br>断然拒绝该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board rebuffed the offer without even reading the full proposal.  董事会甚至没读完提案就断然拒绝了该报价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refuse</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，不肯；废弃物</td><td>refuse to cooperate<br>拒绝合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness refused to answer any further questions in court.  证人拒绝在法庭上回答任何进一步的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repulse</td><td>/rɪˈpʌls/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使厌恶，排斥</td><td>repulsed by the smell<br>被那气味恶心到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sight of the rotting fruit repulsed everyone standing near the crate.  腐烂水果的样子让站在箱子旁的每个人都感到恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snub</td><td>/snʌb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻视，不理睬</td><td>deliberately snub a colleague<br>故意冷落同事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deliberately snubbed her colleague by ignoring him at the meeting.  她在会上故意不理睬同事，冷落了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spurn</td><td>/spɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摈弃，拒绝</td><td>spurn an offer<br>断然拒绝提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spurned the generous offer without giving any explanation at all.  她断然拒绝了这个优厚的提议，且不作任何解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>recalcitrant</b></td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incompliant</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不依从的</td><td>an incompliant witness<br>不肯配合的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incompliant staff refused to follow the new dress rule.  不顺从的员工拒绝遵守新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 governable</del></td><td>/ˈɡʌvərnəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可管理的，易统治的</td><td>a governable class<br>一个好管的班级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With patience the once wild horse became perfectly governable.  在耐心训练下，这匹曾野性难驯的马变得十分听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ruly</del></td><td>/ˈruli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守规矩的，服管的</td><td>a ruly classroom<br>守规矩的教室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher was surprised to find such a ruly classroom.  新老师惊讶地发现这个教室如此守规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>recant</b></td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abjure</td><td>/əbˈdʒʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃；避免</td><td>abjure violence<br>发誓弃绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group publicly abjured violence as a political tool.  该团体公开宣布放弃以暴力作为政治手段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forswear</td><td>/fɔrˈswer/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃，弃绝</td><td>forswear alcohol<br>发誓戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forswore gambling after losing all his savings.  输掉全部积蓄后他发誓戒赌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renege</td><td>/rɪˈniːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>背弃（承诺），反悔</td><td>renege on a promise<br>背弃承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reneged on its promise to raise wages.  公司背弃了加薪的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retract</td><td>/rɪˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>收回，否认</td><td>retract a statement<br>撤回一项声明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper agreed to retract the claim and print a formal apology.  报社同意收回那项说法并刊登正式道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdraw</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取出（款项）；撤回，撤退</td><td>withdraw money from the bank<br>从银行取钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stopped at the bank to withdraw money before the long trip.  他在长途旅行前去银行取钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adhere</td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insist</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>坚持，坚决主张</td><td>insist on an apology<br>坚持要求道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She insisted that the money had been returned last week.  她坚称那笔钱上周已经归还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 maintain</td><td>/meɪnˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>坚持主张；维持，保养</td><td>maintain his innocence<br>坚称自己无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He continued to maintain that he had never seen the document.  他一直坚称自己从未见过那份文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stick</td><td>/ˈstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>坚持，不改口；粘住</td><td>stick to his story<br>坚持他的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under hours of questioning the witness stuck to her original account.  在数小时的盘问下，证人仍坚持她最初的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>recessive</b></td><td>/rɪˈsesɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐性的；内敛的</td><td>a recessive gene<br>隐性基因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His recessive nature kept him silent at every noisy family gathering.  他内敛的性格使他在每次热闹的家庭聚会上都沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 backward</td><td>/ˈbækwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向后的；落后的；羞怯的</td><td>a backward glance<br>回头一望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too backward to introduce himself to the newcomers.  他太腼腆，不敢向新来的人自我介绍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bashful</td><td>/ˈbæʃfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>羞怯的，害羞的</td><td>a bashful smile<br>一个羞涩的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bashful boy hid behind his mother whenever guests arrived.  每次有客人来，这个害羞的男孩就躲到母亲身后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coy</td><td>/kɔɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>羞怯的；不愿明说的</td><td>coy about her past<br>对自己的过去讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was oddly coy about where she had spent the previous summer.  她对上一个夏天去了哪里出奇地讳莫如深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demure</td><td>/dɪˈmjʊr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>端庄的，娴静的</td><td>a demure smile<br>娴静的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a demure nod and said nothing more.  她端庄地点了点头，没再多说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diffident</td><td>/ˈdɪfɪdənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏自信的，羞怯的</td><td>a diffident newcomer<br>腼腆的新人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too diffident to argue with his famous professor.  他太拘谨，不敢与那位名教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 introverted</td><td>/ˈɪntroʊˌvɜːrtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向的，含蓄的</td><td>an introverted child<br>一个内向的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Being introverted, she preferred a quiet library to a noisy party.  由于性格内向，她宁愿待在安静的图书馆而不是喧闹的派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retiring</td><td>/rɪˈtaɪrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>腼腆的，不爱交际的</td><td>a shy and retiring man<br>一个害羞内敛的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author was a retiring man who avoided interviews.  这位作家性格内敛，从不接受采访。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-effacing</td><td>/sɛlf ɪˈfeɪsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不爱出头的，谦逊低调的</td><td>a self-effacing scientist<br>一位低调谦逊的科学家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The self-effacing author refused to appear on television.  这位低调的作家拒绝上电视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheepish</td><td>/ˈʃiːpɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腼腆的；难为情的</td><td>a sheepish grin<br>一个不好意思的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a sheepish apology for forgetting our appointment again.  他为再次忘记约会难为情地道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdrawn</td><td>/wɪθˈdrɔːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向孤僻的，不与人来往的</td><td>become withdrawn<br>变得孤僻内向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy grew quiet and withdrawn after moving to the city.  搬到城里后，那男孩变得沉默孤僻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extroverted</td><td>/ˈekstrəˌvɜːrtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外向的，性格开朗的</td><td>an extroverted student<br>外向的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her extroverted nature made her popular at every new school.  她外向的性格让她在每所新学校都很受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immodest</td><td>/ɪˈmɑdɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不谦虚的，自负的；不检点的</td><td>an immodest boast<br>一句自负的吹牛话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems immodest to list every award on the first page.  把每个奖项都列在第一页显得有些不谦虚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 outgoing</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌɡoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外向的，友好健谈的</td><td>an outgoing personality<br>外向的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His outgoing personality helped him make friends very quickly.  他外向的性格让他很快就交到朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>recidivate</b></del></td><td>/rɪˈsɪdəveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>回到原先的习惯，尤指重新犯罪</td><td>recidivate after release<br>获释后重新犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many offenders recidivate within a year of release without proper support.  许多罪犯在缺乏适当支持时会在获释一年内重新犯罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regress</td><td>/rɪˈɡres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退化，恶化</td><td>regress to childish behavior<br>退回到幼稚行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under stress he tends to regress to childish and sulky behavior.  在压力下他往往退化为幼稚而怨怼的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relapse</td><td>/rɪˈlæps/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>重蹈覆辙，再犯</td><td>relapse into old habits<br>重犯旧习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without support many patients relapse into old habits within months.  缺乏支持的话，许多病人几个月内就重蹈旧习。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 retrogress</del></td><td>/ˌrɛtrəˈɡrɛs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退步，倒退</td><td>retrogress to barbarism<br>倒退回野蛮状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without steady funding the program began to retrogress noticeably.  缺乏稳定资金，这个项目开始明显倒退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revert</td><td>/rɪˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恢复原状，回复</td><td>revert to type<br>回到本性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After a week of dieting he reverted to eating late-night snacks.  节食一周后，他又回到深夜吃零食的老样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 habilitate</del></td><td>/həˈbɪlɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使具备能力，使胜任</td><td>habilitate disabled workers<br>使残障工人具备工作能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The center exists to habilitate young adults with learning difficulties.  该中心旨在使有学习障碍的青年具备生活能力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 redeem</td><td>/rɪˈdiːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改过自新</td><td>redeem oneself through service<br>以服务改过自新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hoped to redeem himself through years of quiet public service.  他希望通过多年默默的公共服务来改过自新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regenerate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重获新生，使焕然一新</td><td>regenerate a neighborhood<br>使一个街区焕然一新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city spent millions to regenerate a neighborhood near the old port.  该市花费数百万使旧港附近的一个街区焕然一新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rehabilitate</td><td>/ˌriːhəˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复原，使康复</td><td>rehabilitate former inmates<br>帮助刑满释放者重返社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program aims to rehabilitate former inmates and find them jobs.  该项目旨在帮刑满释放者重返社会并找到工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reciprocate</b></td><td>/rɪˈsɪprəkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>报答，回报</td><td>reciprocate her kindness<br>回报她的好意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They drove the neighbors to the airport to reciprocate her kindness.  他们开车送邻居去机场，以回报她的好意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recompense</td><td>/ˈrekəmˌpens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>补偿，报酬</td><td>recompense for the loss<br>对损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a small sum as recompense for her trouble.  她得到一小笔钱作为麻烦的补偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repay</td><td>/riˈpeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>偿还；报答</td><td>repay a favor<br>报答一份恩情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I can never repay the patience my mentor showed me.  导师给我的耐心，我永远无法回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requite</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>酬谢，报答；报仇</td><td>requite his loyalty<br>报答他的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted to requite her kindness with a gift she would truly treasure.  他想用一件她会真正珍视的礼物报答她的善意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retaliate</td><td>/rɪˈtælieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>报复，反击</td><td>retaliate against critics<br>报复批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If we raise tariffs, our trading partners will almost certainly retaliate.  如果我们提高关税，贸易伙伴几乎肯定会报复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 return</td><td>/rɪˈtɜːrn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>回报，报答；归还；返回</td><td>return the favor<br>回报这份人情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She helped me move, so I returned the favor with dinner.  她帮我搬家，所以我请她吃饭作为回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 owe</td><td>/oʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>欠（债）；应感谢，归功于</td><td>owe her a debt<br>欠她一份情</td></tr>
